## ☀️ MORNING REPORT 25 Oct (Opus, the build order run: 24 Oct 17:23 to 23:25 UTC, then Parts 3 and 4 on your "keep working" to 03:47). Read this first.

**LIVE (every deploy proven on both origins, from browser profiles that held the old worker):**

- **Pop N Lock v1.7** (Part 0, the real bugs: HYPE works), **v1.8** (Part 1, Astra's art in the match), **v1.9** (Part 2a, the new two row controls), **v1.10** (Part 2b: switching apps now PAUSES Solo and Campaign, a landing guide that shows where pods really end, the incoming Chaff as a number, chains on time). **v1.11** (Part 3, live 03:05 UTC: every word in the game 12 CSS px or more on every phone, new settings Reduce motion, Screen shake, Larger text and Vibration, a HUD that lays itself out at any text size, danger shown as an X too, not only red). **v1.12** (Part 4, live 03:47 UTC: the first three rivals teach one skill each, pop, chain and cancel, on the intro card, in the count and after a loss, until you have shown it; every record now stores its rules, settings and the rival's numbers).
- Every part had its own review; the reviews found real faults in every one of them before it went live, all fixed (details in each entry below).

**Three things wrong in my latest shots (Pop N Lock v1.11, 412 and an iPhone SE in Safari):** the danger X covers the face of the pod that spawns under it (only its rim shows the colour); the incoming Chaff rocks are still tiny beside their number; on the smallest phones with Larger text the button words touch their edges (the page has no side padding) and the outro's lines run over the dimmed HYPE plate. Older and still true: "6 cancelled" sits up in the header, away from its board.

**Not shipped, and why:**
- **Pit Bike Parts 2 to 5:** Part 2 (spec trials) needs medal times nobody has recorded, and the plan says never invent them: your call or a session of real laps. Parts 3 and 4 lean on it, Part 5 is queued behind them, so I moved to Pop N Lock as the order says.
- **Pop N Lock Parts 3 and 4: SHIPPED as v1.11 and v1.12** after you said keep working; each review found real faults, all fixed (Part 4's: Reset campaign progress froze the next match; the cancel lesson pointed at the wrong pair). **The Pop N Lock build order is complete** except Retro (your call). Also not built: a save that survives the phone killing the game in the background (Part 2b only pauses in memory).
- **Super Slice Journey kit:** waiting on Astra's zip, as the order says.
- **Your Super Slice expansion and Tally zips:** not in `assets/` yet, so the Fable handoff is not written. Upload them there and say go.

**Your calls, most important first:**
1. **Pit Bike medal times:** give a ratio for gold and platinum, or let me record skilled laps on a test build. It unblocks three parts.
2. **Pop N Lock landing window:** I made a pair wait at least the stage's own fall before it locks (Astra wrote a flat 300 ms, which locked stage 1's pairs four times sooner than v1.8). Say "flat 300" and it is one number.
3. **Pop N Lock HYPE bonus:** the ALL CITY HYPE +500 now actually pays (20 to 43 percent of a solo score, Sunbeams still capped daily). Keep it, slow it, or keep it out of Sunbeams.
4. **Super Slice Endless:** with phone sight the near human controller reaches 4.7 km in the middle of 20 runs and never 12 km; the 60 speed cap and the hazard ramp set that. Is 12 km meant to be reachable?
5. **Pop N Lock difficulty (measured, 200 seeded matches a rival):** the plan targets 80, 70 and 60 percent wins on the first three rivals. A bot playing like a beginner (a move every 1.2 s, 45 percent blunders) wins 97.5, 94.5 and 81.5; a competent one wins all. They are far gentler than the targets. Harder means touching the rivals' tuning (not their speed), which the plan leaves to you: keep them gentle (the lessons are there now), or say harder.
6. **Pop N Lock, new:** a song earned mid match now shows at the next game start, not on the result screen (a card there would come back after every match until opened). Want it on the result screen anyway? And the music button says "on" beside a button that says "Play beat": fine, or both as actions?
7. Smaller: Pit Bike worn parts still slow the bike in the career and trials; Pit Bike brake dabs still fire nitro about a quarter of the time; Daily medals pay once ever; Pop N Lock's painted Chaff has no face; her shape marks swapped (red diamond, purple star) and Tag shapes does nothing with her pods; six pests have no dance yet (her 105 missing files); Big controls is now off by default; Pop N Lock: a mutual top out is now a DRAW (replayed like a loss, no series point); Astra's rhythm wants the squash to finish before the next pop (as built, chains run about 80 ms a link quicker); the floats still shout with exclamation marks (Part 3's copy pass).

**Reviews:** every part today had one or two independent reviews, and every one of them found real faults before the deploy (Super Slice G: 7, Pit Bike v1.9: three rounds, Pop N Lock v1.8: 5, v1.9: 3, v1.10: 3). Each fix has a check that goes red when broken.

**⏸ 24 Oct 23:24 UTC (Opus) POP N LOCK v1.10 LIVE: FEEL AND FAIRNESS** (main `bf807d7a4`, both origins proven from profiles that held v1.9). Switching to another app now PAUSES Solo and Campaign (it used to play the match on without you and could lose it), and coming back counts 3, 2, 1 with the controls held. The landing guide now shows where each pod really ends up, the far one too when a pair lands across two columns of different heights (Outline, Solid or Off in Settings). Incoming Chaff reads as a number with a big rock per row, and a chain that cancels some shows "6 cancelled" for a moment. Chains run on time: a fall takes 110 to 180 ms by its height and every landing squashes for a beat (the old fall shaved a share each frame, so a 120 Hz phone played every chain slower). Both boards finish their move before a top out is judged, so the player's board no longer loses a tie just by being checked first: both on the same tick is a draw, replayed like a loss. Two review rounds (the first caught the count letting you play during 3, 2, 1, a draw stamped DEFEAT, a juddering end collapse, and Chaff hanging over your score); 12 checks, 14 broken copies red. **Three things wrong in my shots:** the queue's rocks are tiny beside its number; "cancelled" sits up in the header away from the board; the guide's connector line crosses other pods and the pivot's dot is nearly invisible. **Your calls:** (1) a mutual top out is a DRAW (stage replayed, no series point): fine, or should one side win?; (2) Astra's rhythm reads squash, then pop; as built the next pop starts as the squash begins (about 80 ms quicker per chain link): wait the extra 80 ms or keep it brisk?; (3) a phone that kills the game in the background still loses the match: a save that survives that (board, queue, seed and clocks) is not built.

**🕹 24 Oct 22:43 UTC (Opus) POP N LOCK v1.9 LIVE: THE NEW CONTROLS** (main `57c949749`, both origins proven from profiles that held v1.8). Astra's two row tray: LEFT, SOFT, RIGHT over ROTATE L, ROTATE R, DROP, every button 56 px on every phone, under the board instead of on top of it. A held arrow repeats on one clock (your July 105 then 33 ms; Relaxed is a setting), a turn pressed between pairs turns the next one, DROP works with your other thumb still down and only ever drops the pair you pressed it on, a drag on the board picks sideways or down and sticks to it, and the flick up to drop is now a setting, off (it dropped pairs by accident). New: a landing window, so a pair that touches down waits before it locks (you can still slide or turn it twice), and a fixed rotation rule (a pair can no longer hop up a wall when it turns). Settings gained Relaxed held moves, Flick up to drop, and Drop button on the left. Two review rounds; 34 checks, 25 broken copies red. **Three things wrong in my shots:** at 412 there is still a 90 px dead band above the board (Part 3's bigger grid should use it); the tray's labels are a dim grey on dark brown; the tray cuts the alley's crates and boombox in half. **Your calls:** (1) the landing window waits at least the stage's own fall, not a flat 300 ms as Astra wrote (a flat 300 locked stage 1's pairs four times sooner than v1.8 did, against your August "too fast" notes): say flat 300 and it is one number; (2) Big controls is now off by default (56 px rows; on makes them 64), because the plan says 56 and 64 shrinks the board; (3) on phones with browser bars showing (an iPhone in Safari) the match is about 9 percent smaller than v1.8, because two rows of 56 px need the height: Part 3's layout pass is where that comes back.

**🕺 24 Oct 21:33 UTC (Opus) POP N LOCK v1.8 LIVE: ASTRA'S ART IS IN THE MATCH** (main `30e027b49`, both origins proven from profiles that held v1.7). Her painted pods with their shape marks, joins between touching pods of a colour, her board frames (the corner speakers kept round), her next window, and the opponent's rail: the dance plays once when the rival hits a chain of 2 and holds the pose until the chain ends, a hit pose for 180 ms when Chaff lands on them, a danger pose when their middle column is 10 high. Only the current opponent's frames and portraits stay in memory (the old build kept every pest's portraits, 19 MB by the end of the ladder). The review caught five faults before it went live, all fixed: the next pair sat on the alley graffiti, the HYPE bar covered the frame's right edge, the incoming Chaff rocks sat on the frame's top band, the dance replayed and flashed the old portrait in long chains, and the speakers drew as squashed ovals. 15 checks, 14 broken copies red. **Three things wrong in my shots:** the next pair is drawn tiny (pods half the board's size) in her big window; the incoming Chaff now sits in the header beside your score, which reads less clearly as "coming at you" (Part 2b redraws it as a number); the old one row tray still nicks the frame's bottom speakers (Part 2a's tray fixes it). **Your calls:** (1) the painted Chaff rock has no face: the code face's dark eyes vanish into its crater, so a face needs Astra (or white eyes, your taste); (2) her shape marks moved: red now carries the diamond and purple the star (the July build had them the other way), and the Tag shapes setting does nothing while her pods show (the marks are always on); (3) six pests have no dance yet (her 105 missing files).

**🕺 24 Oct 20:30 UTC (Opus) POP N LOCK v1.7 LIVE: THE HYPE METER WORKS FOR THE FIRST TIME, AND FIVE REAL BUGS ARE GONE** (main `0d841452e`, both origins proven from profiles that held v1.6). Astra found them, I verified and fixed them: HYPE drained 80 points in 61 ms because the game read milliseconds as seconds (it never showed; now it falls 1.3 a second and holds), the rival's chain glow likewise; the All Clear bonus was spent on a plain lock instead of your next chain; a touch the phone cancelled could rotate or hard drop your piece; a lock in the middle of a soft drop drag carried the fast fall onto the next piece; a held arrow's first repeat came at 138 ms instead of 105; the next pair now sits inside its painted window. One more I found while testing: starting a soft drop on an early stage could drop the piece up to 20 rows in one frame. The review caught my first fix slowing the soft drop by a quarter, so it now keeps every stage's fall rate exactly. With HYPE alive, the ALL CITY HYPE banner shows up often, so it now fits the board instead of running off it. 11 checks, 9 broken copies red. **Three things wrong in my shots:** the HYPE meter is a thin unlabelled bar beside the board (nobody will know what it is); the falling pair's top pod hides under the board's top frame as it spawns; the landing ghost's dark yellow disc reads as a hole. **Your calls:** (1) the ALL CITY HYPE +500 bonus now actually pays (20 to 43 percent of a solo score, and solo Sunbeams come faster, still under the daily cap): keep it, slow it, or leave the bonus out of Sunbeams; (2) a tap now has 105 ms (it had 138) before the held repeat starts, as the plan asked: worth a feel on your phone; (3) the game's floats still shout ("ALL CITY HYPE!", "DENIED!", older copy with exclamation marks). **NEXT:** Part 1, Astra's art wired in.

**🏍 24 Oct 20:25 UTC (Opus) PIT BIKE v1.9 LIVE: THE ANALOG THROTTLE (YOUR IDEA) AND A NITRO THAT NEVER NEEDS THE GAS THUMB TO LIFT** (main `ff1162426`, both origins byte compared, the previous build still served for open tabs, a fresh load boots the new build). A stick appears under your right thumb: pull down for gas, from nothing to full by how far (half throttle is a real coast); push up and hold for the brake (touch never had one, so the brake parts finally do something on a phone); flick up and back for nitro, and the gas comes back to where your thumb settles. Your left NITRO button sits top left for the steering thumb. New players start on the stick; an existing save keeps the GAS button and is offered the new throttle once; the pause menu switches any time. **It took three review rounds:** the first build fired nitro on a hard brake or a quick lift, the second on a brake that eased while held; now a flick has to turn at once and come back to the gas side. Also in: RESTART RACE no longer turns a career round into a paid free race (older than v1.8), it keeps today's 2x featured race, and the Daily no longer races yesterday's ghost. 39 checks with real touches, 21 broken copies red. **Three things wrong in my shots:** at the start line the steer stick sits right over your tiny bike; six nitro pips are hard to count at a glance; the GAS label hides under your thumb exactly while you use it. **Your calls:** (1) a brake dab with almost no rest still fires nitro about a quarter of the time (a dab with no rest IS a flick); your left NITRO is the sure way; (2) worn parts still slow the bike in the career and trials (only NEW wear stopped); (3) Daily medals pay once ever, not once a day (older); (4) **Part 2 (spec trials on a loan bike) needs gold and platinum times on a stock bike:** the only recorded stock laps are the autopilot's, which set bronze and silver as today; a recorded skilled lap per track (yours or your brother's, on a test build) or your ratio unblocks it, and the Spec Cup and mastery stars wait with it.

## ☀️ MORNING REPORT, 24 Oct: SUPER SLICE 3D PART E IS LIVE, ALL FOUR DEPLOYS (read this first)

Overnight Opus run, from about 22:45 to 05:15 UTC by the box clock. Build **v6.8**, portal card `?v=20261024d`, probed on lucidwinds.com and www, and proven on browser profiles that held the previous version. **A phone must close the tab once.**

**What is live**
- **E1 (v6.5): stacks by a wall, and every run paid once.** A stack hugging a wall no longer machine guns the knife: it bonks once, the wall bounces it, and the blade carves the column on the way down. Slivers, Sunbeams and the clean dive count are paid exactly once per run (finished twice, two tabs, a reload), and a fail still pays.
- **E2 (v6.6): Clean Slice and finds.** A numbered dive names a line of stacks, each with a gold collar on its top slab; carve them all and land for the Clean Slice badge ("Clean Slice 12 of 17" otherwise). A near human controller carved every line over hundreds of real dives. About half of dives get a line. One slab on the line holds a seed in its world's hat (drawn in code), told once on the end panel. Fewer debris and particles; a world's paintings leave the phone's GPU when you leave it.
- **E3 (v6.7): the Daily.** A Daily button on the title: the same dive on every phone each UTC day, one scored try (used up when it starts, never by opening the card), practice as often as you like, a countdown to the next day, and the Daily badge for reaching the floor. A Daily never moves a level.
- **E4 (v6.8): the bosses.** Levels 5, 10 ... 40 now end at the world's boss in a chamber below where the floor was (the shaft above is the very same dive). Fall onto it blade first for a hit; it hops one lane over and the net under it lobs you back onto it; three hits and it opens (Astra's three stages, with a drawn crack after the first), +600, and on to the floor. A handle bounces off it. Miss three lobs and the net gives way; cut a rope to skip it; either skip costs the Clean Slice. The How page explains it in one sentence.
- **Proof, every part:** the full suite green (211 checks now), every new check watched going red on a broken copy (E1 22 groups, E2 26, E3 22, E4 28), a review in two or three lenses with a skeptic per finding and everything real fixed before the ship, and the live build checked on both origins from browser profiles that still held the previous version.

**What I saw in the shots (three things wrong, before you find them)**
1. The boss's rope tags are flat bright yellow rectangles: they read as placeholder blocks, not gold tags, and with the boss in a side lane the tag overlaps the edge of the boss (the net and ropes are drawn in code; Astra drew none).
2. With the boss in a side lane, a 412 px screen cuts part of it off at the edge until the knife comes near: the chase camera follows the knife, not the fight.
3. A boss win pays a lot: the shot's end panel reads 7,700 and +201 Slivers, nearly all of it the 600 multiplied by the x10 bullseye the knife always drops onto after the third hit (call 2 below). Smaller: the "hit 1 of 3" popup sits on the knife's handle, and the net only answers a landing with a few white sparks.
The Daily's card, its spent state, the four button title row and its end panel looked right at 412 x 915; the end panel's confetti still crosses the buttons, as it always has.

**What I left unshipped, and why**
- A looser Clean Slice corridor that gives nearly every dive a line: 5 of 80 of its lines were never carved by the controller, so it would name lines nobody can finish. Held; about half of dives have no line.
- A shared Daily board (everyone's score for the day) needs a server. Results are personal, and the card says so.
- Part F, held for your word and untouched: the supporter shelf and prices, the cross game entitlement, the six new earned skins' prices, Deep Tide's gravity, six authored courses per world, seasonal remixes, the beehive, the seed audience (SS36).

**Found and not fixed (yours to know)**
- A knife falling into the 1.4 u gap between two gate slabs is bonked every step until it cuts one (up to about 28 wood sounds in 0.23 s).
- A knife stuck in a wall can still be nudged about 0.1 u by a slab bonk in the same step.
- The fall beside a wall stack now carves the whole column with its combo (about 660 points at x10 with no input at all).
- Two tabs that both tap Scored before either one's first tick both get a scored Daily (it needs two tabs inside about a second).

**Your calls**
1. **The boss fight's feel.** The net aims each lob at the boss. A player who leaves it alone or taps lightly (0.1 s or less) clears every fight; one who holds toward the boss for 0.15 s or more on EVERY lob overshoots every time and the net gives way (nobody dies, nothing softlocks; correcting only when a lob would miss clears 200 of 200). The How page says "the net aims for you, so steer lightly". Keep that, or make the lob forgive a steering thumb?
2. **The x10 after a win.** The third hit is always in the middle lane, so the knife then drops onto the x10 bullseye, and the 600 is multiplied like every reward earned in a dive. A review skeptic measured about 10 percent more score than a player who aims for the bullseye anyway. Keep it, or end the fight in a side lane, or pay the 600 after the multiplier?
3. **Should the net giving way cost the Clean Slice?** The plan names only the rope, and calls the boss optional for Clean Slice. As built, both skips forfeit it. Flip it so a lost fight keeps the badge?
4. **The ropes.** A rope now cuts only on a lob, and never the rope on the side the boss has hopped to (a tap toward the boss used to cut that one in 35 of 40 played fights). The reviewer's other option: ropes nearer the walls (then only the middle lane reaches one).
5. **The plan's two bumpers became a net**, so no knife can fall past the boss before the fight is decided; and the chamber sits below the old floor so nothing above it changed.
6. **Daily practice pays** like any dive (Sunbeams under the cap, Slivers by score). Say if practice should pay nothing.
7. **Astra asks:** eight seed in a hat paintings for the finds; the boss's net and ropes are drawn in code too.

**🔪 24 Oct (Opus) SUPER SLICE 3D PART E4 LIVE = v6.8: THE BOSSES. PART E IS DONE** (deploy `468221c63`). Each world's last dive (levels 5, 10 ... 40) now ends at its boss, in a chamber below where the floor used to be (the shaft above is the very same dive). Fall onto it blade first for a hit; it hops one lane over and the net under it lobs you back onto it; three hits and it opens (Astra's three stages, a drawn crack after the first), then the net drops and you go on to the floor. A handle bounces off it. Miss three lobs and the net gives way; cut a rope (with the boss in the middle) to skip it; either skip costs the Clean Slice. A review found real faults, all fixed before the ship (the worst: a tap toward the boss after it hopped carried the knife into a rope and skipped the fight). See the morning report below for your calls on the feel.

**🔪 24 Oct (Opus) SUPER SLICE 3D PART E3 LIVE = v6.7** (deploy `366daab0b`). **The Daily:** a Daily button on the title opens today's dive, the same dive on every phone in the world (seeded by the date in UTC). ## ☀️ MORNING REPORT, 23 Oct: THE RABBIT RONIN LEVEL BUILD OUT IS LIVE, ALL FOUR PARTS (read this first)

Overnight Opus run, 03:38 to 05:35 UTC by the box clock. Build **v2.15**, portal card `?v=20261023j`, probed on lucidwinds.com and www. **A phone must close the tab once.**

**What is live**
- **Five places in every dojo.** Each dojo is now The Approach, The Works, The Gallery, The Climb and The Gauntlet, in an order that rotates every dojo (dojo 1 keeps that order for its teaching). The Approach is a warm up with small hops, a row of three cages on the ground and the most carrots of the dojo. The Works is the world's crates, tunnels, ledges or terraces, doubled, with cages on top of them and inside the tunnels. The Gallery is a chain of two to four swings with crows in the corridor and cages on high ledges. The Climb is a stair of ledges up to a plateau, cages up the stair, then a cannon or a pad where it drops. The Gauntlet is the world's enemy mix, one swing, cages on the hedgehogs' beats. The diamond waits on a raised dais. Running, jumping, swinging, the stick and the pit widths are untouched.
- **The light changes as you go.** Each rest post turns the hour: the yards from dusk to lantern night (the post lanterns glow), the burrows from torchlight to deep dark to a gold exit light, the grove from noon to a green dusk with fireflies, the peaks from dawn fog to bright blue to a red sunset. One sun or moon per sky: three worlds used to show two moons at once.
- **A named landmark at every rest post.** The Crate Torii, Lantern Mast (a red banner), Rope Bridge; the Root Arch, Glow Ring, Old Burrow; the Shrine Stone, Lantern Tree, Falls; the Cairn Tower, Lone Pine, Prayer Flags. Passing a post says "Rest post 2 of 4 at the Lantern Mast", the pause panel says where you rested, the journal lists each dojo's posts.
- **One job per dojo.** A card names it as the dojo starts (it never holds the controls), a line under the score tracks it, the clear banner tallies it, and the first time it is done a badge goes on the dojo map with 10 carrots. Six kinds rotate and grow: free every mouse; free every mouse without losing a heart; reach the diamond inside a par time; swing 2 or 3 rings in a row without touching the ground; cut 3 to 6 hedgehogs; clear without losing a heart. Stars unchanged.
- **Proof:** the solver clears all 24 dojos; a scripted thumb frees all 360 cages (and catches a cage I planted out of reach); every job kind was completed through the engine and banks once; the ring jobs were done by real swinging; the light costs about a millisecond a frame. Two independent reviews read the work after it shipped and found thirteen real faults between them (the worst: the Works built one structure where it asked for eight, a pad in dojo 1 bounced you into spikes, the job line was unreadable on the bright skies, the dojo card flashed by in under 2 s on a 120 Hz phone like your Pixel). All thirteen are fixed and live.

**What I saw in the shots (three things wrong, before you find them)**
1. The painted scenery was painted for night. In the brightest hours (grove noon, peaks bright blue) it reads as dark cutouts against a bright sky, and the lanterns painted into the yards' scenery stay dark at night while the rest post lanterns glow.
2. The twelve landmarks are a first pass in drawn shapes over Astra's painted world. They read as places, but they look drawn, not painted (the Lantern Tree most of all). A good next Astra ask: paint the twelve.
3. The three narrowest Galleries (dojos 1, 2 and 15) still have one or two cages on the ground: there is no room for a ledge between the swings without moving the swings.

**What I left unshipped, and why**
- The plan's "collect every carrot" job: about one carrot in five sits on a high bonus ledge only a nearby cannon reaches, so in some dojos it could be impossible. A job must always be doable, so it is held; it could come back as "collect 9 in 10 carrots" if you want it.
- Section E (a secret pocket per dojo, Astra's challenge dojos 25 to 32): your call, untouched.
- The plan's 60 fps gate as written: this box draws the canvas in software, where even the old v2.9 takes 23 to 64 ms a frame at phone resolution, so no build can pass it here. I measured what the light adds instead: 0 to 1.4 ms. Worth one look on the phone.

**Your calls when you have played it:** the five places, the light arcs, the landmark names, the job list, and whether to send Astra the landmarks.

**🎸 18 Oct HIS FRETWORK NOTE, VERBATIM: "i also want to have a pro tips part in there that kind of appears or you can read and see examples. one really great tip is how usic is all about inversions and tension resolution. when putting together chord progressions, to make them flow and feel liek theyre walking and are cohesive, you have to look not just at the chords but the common tones among them. so oftent heres only one or two not difference, keeping those common tones and moving the the one or two notes as small of a distance as possible is how a lot of peorfessionals create strong lines and connections. thats one example."** Sort: new feature (v21): a Pro tips section to read, each tip with a live example on the neck, tips that surface once where they apply; tip 1 = voice leading by common tones, his words. ## ⛔⛔ URGENT, NEXT SESSION: PLAYERS LOSE WHAT THEY UNLOCKED WHEN BROWSER DATA IS CLEARED (Stephen, Sep 19 2026)

**His words:** "I cleared the last 15 minutes of my browser data and I lost my unlocked songs in my whole game studio and
everything so that's going to need to be addressed immediately so players don't lose stuff. We're going to need a better
way to back up their account information to their phone or to our servers or something, but it needs to be saved for
people so when they unlock stuff it stays unlocked. Even if they clear browser data and stuff that they shouldn't be
losing stuff."

**What is known (checked Sep 19, not yet designed):**
- Music unlocks live ONLY in the browser: `music-unlocks.js` keeps `localStorage.sws_music_progress` (the source of
  truth, per game slug) and `sws_game_unlocks` (the ledger, rebuilt from it). Clearing site data for lucidwinds.com
  erases both; nothing restores them. (The file mentions a "cloud restore" only for `sws_music_revealed`; find what
  that is before assuming any cloud copy exists.)
- "The whole game studio" = the other games' progress, also per-browser storage (localStorage / IndexedDB per game).
  Tiny World's worlds (IndexedDB `tw`) have the same exposure: its only backup today is ☰ Save to file.
- Firebase exists (project focus-grove-fffa8, Auth + Firestore `vaults/{uid}`) for Lucid Winds itself; whether the
  arcade games can use it, and how a player signs in without friction (kids' games: no required account), is the design.

**What the next session must do first:** list every key the arcade games write that a player would miss (unlocks,
progress, worlds, settings), then propose ONE fleet-wide backup (server copy tied to an account and/or a device
backup file / passkey) to Stephen before building. Laws that apply: no approval gates in play, nothing paywalled,
STRIPE ONLY on the web, kids' privacy (no personal data without need).

### The Sep 20 pass, for the record

Nine notes from his own play plus three from his tester, all fixed, deployed and looked at. Truth is
`/workspaces/tiny-world/STATUS.md` (a table of every note, what it really was, and the flag it sits behind).

The five that mattered most, because each was a whole class and not one bug:
1. **The tray could never put gear down at all**, so no combo that needs A next to B was reachable from the
   tray. That is why every hat failed on the snowman. And the opening scene ate its own snowman before the
   first frame, because the hat was inside the reaction's radius.
2. **Meat eaters starve where they stand**: a hunter has no food thing in the world and could see ten tiles.
3. **The whole game could hold only ONE dawn row**, so the troll turning to stone could not be built as data
   even if someone had tried. Fixed, and trolls now do it.
4. **The catapult was unreachable by every player action**, and its fixture passed on a phantom event only a
   brand new world gives. The dog's toy, the bird's crown and the bounce pad all announced themselves and did
   nothing at all.
5. **A newborn's breeding cooldown was zero**, so two people became sixty in ten minutes.

Second pass added: the fence goes both ways from one tray tile (his tester's note 3); a reaction that reaches
nothing steps aside instead of claiming the trigger, which is what let one row swallow every storm row and
announce itself 469 times in a world with no lava; the lute and lantern keep working instead of firing once; the
chef cooks; cooking means something; the scarecrow guards a field; the menu has an X; the place hints stopped
lying about dragging; the News setting was a dead box until you tapped Sounds first.

⛔ STILL OPEN: `sim-coverage` red at 9 blocks (25 before this session, 67 with the old scenes against the new
code); seven of the nine are C2's storm internals. The opening screen is still props scattered on a lawn with
hard rectangular terrain edges. Eight more items ranked in the handoff.

shot ever taken of this game was an empty green lawn. Use `dev/look-sizes.mjs` or no query.

## 0f. HIS NOTE 21:00 UTC Sep 17, VERBATIM: "i just got a complaint form someone random that says they cant type games in
from leop in the portal" → **REAL FAULT, FIXED, LIVE 21:20 UTC (main 104d82a8). THE FIRST ORGANIC REPORT FROM THE BIG BUTTON.**
The stored report (Firestore `feedback`, 20:56:05 UTC, Discord ping ok): msg "[bug] I can not type games in — from Leo
[portal]" ("leop" = Leo + the portal tag), game "Not a game", **no contact address**, ua = **desktop Safari 15.6 on macOS
10.15 (a Mac, not a phone)**. Cause: both portal search boxes carried a readonly-until-focus autofill trick (Aug 21,
against saved email autofill). WebKit fixes a field's editability when it takes focus, so lifting readonly inside the
focus handler leaves the box dead until it is focused again; the same trick also blocks the keyboard on iPhone. Headless
Chromium typed fine, so no gate saw it. Trick removed, autofill attributes stay. Gates: `portal/dev/probe-search-typing.mjs`
(readonly false at rest + real tap + real typing, red on the old page, green live) and `portal/dev/probe-feedback-typing.mjs`
(the big button itself opens and takes every letter, 8/8 live). ⛔ Earlier note here said "iPhone"; corrected 21:40 UTC.
Also in the collection, unanswered: Sep 7, a stranger on Android in Sudoku: "Feedback button is in the way of the screen".

**⛔ STILL OPEN, STEPHEN'S, 14:30 UTC: CLOUDFLARE KEEPS 429s IT CACHED DURING THE LOCKOUT.** A live Jimothy load got
`assets/icons/jimothy-192.png` as 429, `cf-cache-status: HIT`, `age` 56636 s, empty body, one year immutable header (the
`.htaccess` image rule stamps it on every status). Only some Cloudflare locations hold one (IAD yes, EWR no), so no probe
from here finds them all. **Fix: Cloudflare dashboard, lucidwinds.com, Caching, Configuration, Purge Everything.** Then,
optionally, a cache rule that keeps no 4xx or 5xx at the edge. Any image that "does not load" for one person and loads for
another is this until that purge is done.

**Hostinger's CDN punishes a visitor's IP address, and that is what "a bunch of shit is broken" has meant since Sep 15.**
It has two moods and they look like different bugs, which is why it keeps getting misdiagnosed:

| mood | what he sees | the tell |
|---|---|---|
| **429 lockout** | Jimothy's art missing, then the arcade and apps page fail for minutes | empty 429 bodies with no MIME type |
| **Tarpit** | A game "flashes and nothing happens". Pages answer **200** but the first byte takes **19.7 s** | the *same* 19.7 s on every path, while `x-hcdn-upstream-rt` reads `0.004` |

**Why a tarpit looks like "nothing happens":** the root `sw.js` navigation handler waits 5 s for the network, tries the cache,
and only gives up at **12 s** with an offline page. On a tarpitted phone a game tap is therefore a flash and then a blank
screen for twelve seconds. Nobody waits twelve seconds. **That is the flash.**

**⛔ CORRECTED 13:10 UTC SEP 16, THE FIX IS NOT IN hPANEL.** lucidwinds.com is on Stephen's OWN Cloudflare zone (NS
ollie/terin.ns.cloudflare.com); hPanel says "Domain isn't connected" because of that. The chain is: his Cloudflare → Hostinger's
CDN (the `x-hcdn-*` layer, THE PUNISHER) → the LiteSpeed origin at **82.25.83.190** (found via the unproxied `ftp.` record).
Probed direct to the origin with `--resolve`: **200 in 58 ms**, full page, valid Let's Encrypt cert for lucidwinds.com (Sep 11 to
Dec 10). His Cloudflare's own edge and cache answer this box in 36 to 47 ms. So everything slow is Hostinger's CDN and nothing else.
**THE FIX: in his Cloudflare dashboard, DNS, point the `lucidwinds.com` and `www` records at A 82.25.83.190, proxy left ON (instant,
no propagation), SSL/TLS mode Full (strict).** That removes Hostinger's CDN from the path. Verify with one probe: no `x-hcdn` headers,
first byte under one second. The old advice below is kept for the record only.

**THE OLD ADVICE (superseded):** hPanel → lucidwinds.com → Performance → CDN → turn down or off the security
layer (rate limiting, DDoS / "under attack", bot protection). Nothing in this repo can serve a page the edge will not hand
over. He has been told this three times; if it is still not done, **ask him for a screenshot of that hPanel page and walk
him through it rather than writing more code.**

⛔ **BEFORE EVER SAYING "THE SITE IS DOWN":** check `time_starttransfer`, check `x-hcdn-upstream-rt`, and fetch the same URL
once from a second egress (WebFetch). A fixed, identical delay across unrelated paths is a rule, not an outage. Three
separate passes on Sep 16 wrote "the whole site is returning 522" about a site that was serving fine.

⛔ **NEVER BURST-PROBE THE LIVE SITE.** It locks this box out too, and then every probe lies. One file at a time, with gaps.

**Re-verified 12:31 UTC Sep 16:** `/portal/` from this box = 200, first byte 19.48 s, `x-hcdn-upstream-rt` 0.013; the same
URL from a second egress loaded promptly. The punishment on an address lasts HOURS (this box was quiet for seven). His
tester's Wi-Fi shares one address, so one Jimothy open punished every game that followed, all afternoon.

## 3. HOW TO CHECK A "GAME IS BROKEN" REPORT IN THE RIGHT ORDER

Do these in order and stop at the first one that explains it. Most reports die at step 1 or 2.

1. **Is it an IN DEVELOPMENT game and does his browser have the flag?** `beta:true` games are gated: without
   `localStorage.sws_dev_ok === '1'` a tap opens a "Tester key" modal instead of the game (`swsDevGate`,
   `portal/index.html`). On a browser he has not unlocked, **every** in-development game refuses to open.
2. **Is the edge tarpitting or locking out?** Section 1. Check TTFB, not just the status code.
3. **Does the page serve, and are the served bytes the built bytes?** `curl` it once with a random query and `diff` the
   result against the file in the tree. A 200 is not proof; a byte diff is.
4. **Does it boot clean from the local copy?** Serve the repo and load the game headless: count requests, 404s and console
   errors. If it boots clean locally and serves byte-identical live, **the game is not broken and the fault is between his
   phone and the file.**
5. **Only then** suspect the game.

**Worked example, Burrow Bowl, Sep 16:** he reported "wouldn't even load, it just flashes and nothing happens". Steps 3 and 4
cleared it: it serves 200 at the exact URL the card uses, byte-identical to the tree, and the local copy boots with **0
console errors, 0 404s and 7 requests**. So Burrow Bowl is not broken. It is step 1 or step 2 on his device, and the
twelve-second blank in section 1 is almost certainly the flash.

## 5. HIS OPEN DECISIONS (taste, not bugs — do not "fix" these unasked)

From the shot sweep, about 110 screenshots across the seven new games at four widths, all written into
`plans/<game>/HANDOFF-<GAME>.md`:

1. **Wordless screens.** Four of seven games have doors with no words, and BRIM's ask lives on the door and never where the
   choosing happens.
2. **One mark doing two jobs.** Reveals mark the child's choice and the true answer identically almost everywhere. NOTCH's
   find and GAUGE's compare already do it right: fill for truth, outline for choice.
3. **TINT's mixer averages dye against white in linear light**, washing every recipe toward grey, in a colour game.
4. **The corner disease.** The first earned thing sits top-left of an empty board in five games.
5. **CORE's settings gear sits 8 px from the right edge**, fleet-wide.
6. **NOTCH shows the browser's blue focus ring** on five screens.
7. **The nine math cards have no art.** On the shelf they are a small emoji in an empty black rectangle beside full-bleed
   painted neighbours, and they read as holes. Nine pieces of card art is the fix and it is his to commission.

## 7. THE LAWS THAT DO NOT BEND

- Commit and push when green. `git add` fenced paths, never `-A`.
- Deploy = a worktree on `origin/main`, `git checkout <branch> -- <paths>`, verify `HEAD~1 == origin/main`, push, then probe
  one served file at a time with a random query. **Never push the whole branch to main**; it carries unfinished games.
- Every gate is watched red on a plant before it counts. Never weaken a gate.
- **A visual change is not done until it has been LOOKED at.** Open the image and name three things wrong in it.
- Player copy: no dashes, no exclamation points. "Sky Wolf Studio", singular. Touch targets 48 px. Text 0.7rem or more.
- Never edit `scripts/`, `music-unlocks.js`, `CLAUDE.md`, `proto/`. Never `pkill -f` a pattern; kill by PID.
